S&P 500's big daily moves (±2% or more), 2000–2026: what was in the sky ±3 days

COMEX ^GSPC, 20 Nov 2000 – 20 Nov 2026: 489 days with a close-to-close move of 2% or more out of 6539 (218 up, 271 down; 44.6% up). For each factor, the number of big days on which it was present (events within ±3 calendar days; states on the day; spx rules active) is compared with the number expected from the same years (big moves cluster in volatile years, so a slow planet that happened to sit in one sign during 2008 or 2013 is not credited). Lift = observed ÷ expected; p = Poisson; q = false-discovery-rate correction over all 698 factors tested — only q < 0.10 should be read as a finding. Big days come in bursts (e.g. March 2020, January 2026), so the episode column shows how many separate occasions stand behind each count; a lift carried by two or three episodes is weaker than the p-value suggests.

Result

  • Any big move (q < 0.10): State: Venus retrograde (×1.75); State: Mars with Jupiter (×1.68); Rule: Jupiter-Neptune 7th (opposition) [Bearish] (×1.63); Rule: Mars-Jupiter conjunction (same sign) [Mixed] (×1.63); Rule: Jupiter-Pluto 5/9 (Trine) (Strong Bullish Permutation Combination) [Bullish] (×1.56); Rule: Jupiter-Herschel 6/8 (Shadashtak) (Strong Bullish Permutation Combination) [Bullish] (×1.42); Rule: Jupiter-Herschel 6/8 (Shadashtak) [Bullish] (×1.42); Rule: Mercury-Saturn 2/12 (Dwidwadash) [Bullish] (×1.38)
  • Big up moves (q < 0.10): none
  • Big down moves (q < 0.10): State: Mars with Jupiter (×1.82); Rule: Mars-Jupiter conjunction (same sign) [Mixed] (×1.77); Rule: Jupiter-Pluto 5/9 (Trine) (Strong Bullish Permutation Combination) [Bullish] (×1.57)

Does it hold out of sample?

Leads picked on 2013 and earlier (lift > 1, p < 0.05 on a side, at least 5 separate episodes) and then measured on the later years: 39 of 87 stayed above 1; pooled 1275 observed against 1234.1 expected (×1.03, p = 0.125).
